A frozen retriever R constructs a candidate pool

$$

where M is the candidate-pool size limit and we set $K _ { i } =$ min $\left( K _ { \operatorname* { m a x } } , | C _ { i } | \right)$

W<sub>e cons</sub>id<sub>er</sub> th<sub>e space</sub> ${ \mathfrak { S } } _ { i }$ <sub>o</sub>f <sub>or</sub>d<sub>ere</sub>d<sub>, non-repea</sub>ti<sub>ng se-</sub> <sub>q</sub>uences <sup>f</sup>rom $C _ { i }$ <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ose</sub> l<sub>eng</sub>th i<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub>t <sub>mos</sub>t $K _ { i }$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ose</sub> <sub>ren</sub>d<sub>ere</sub>d <sub>promp</sub>t <sub>sa</sub>ti<sub>s</sub>fi<sub>es</sub> <sub>a</sub> h<sub>ar</sub>d <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ex</sub>t li<sub>m</sub>it $L _ { \mathrm { m a x } } .$ <sup>A</sup>t ste<sub>p</sub> t the controller holds $\mathbf { S } _ { t } = \left( d _ { 1 } , \ldots , d _ { t } \right)$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>c</sub>h<sub>ooses</sub>

$$

$$

Selecting d appends it to the ordered profile; selecting S<sub>top</sub> t<sub>erm</sub>i<sub>na</sub>t<sub>es</sub> <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> fi<sub>na</sub>l <sub>ou</sub>t<sub>pu</sub>t i<sub>s</sub>

$$

where G is the frozen generator and $P _ { \tau }$ <sup>is</sup> p<sup>rom</sup>p<sup>t</sup> <sup>tem</sup>p<sup>late</sup>.

Mi<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ma</sub>l <sub>su</sub>fi<sub>c</sub>i<sub>en</sub>t <sub>persona</sub>li<sub>za</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> L<sub>e</sub>t $U _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } )$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>cos</sub>t<sub>-</sub>f<sub>ree persona</sub>li<sub>ze</sub>d <sub>u</sub>tilit<sub>y</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> S<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> 2<sub>.</sub>3<sub>, an</sub>d l<sub>e</sub>t

$$

$$

M<sub>u</sub>lti<sub>-</sub>h<sub>ea</sub>d <sub>va</sub>l<sub>ue</sub> <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>ll<sub>er.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> <sub>every</sub> <sub>ava</sub>il<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e</sub> <sub>ac</sub>ti<sub>on,</sub> th<sub>e con</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>ll<sub>er pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>s</sub> th<sub>ree ou</sub>t<sub>come componen</sub>t<sub>s o</sub>f th<sub>e</sub> <sub>ne</sub>t<sub>-op</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>l <sub>samp</sub>l<sub>e</sub>d <sub>con</sub>ti<sub>nua</sub>ti<sub>on:</sub> $Q _ { \theta } ^ { g } ( s , a ) \overset { \textstyle - } { , } Q _ { \theta } ^ { p } ( s , a )$ <sub>,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d $Q _ { \theta } ^ { c } ( s , a )$ <sub>.</sub> Th<sub>ese quan</sub>titi<sub>es es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>e norma</sub>li<sub>ze</sub>d t<sub>as</sub>k <sub>ga</sub>i<sub>n,</sub> <sub>norma</sub>li<sub>ze</sub>d <sub>ma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>h<sub>e</sub>d<sub>-rep</sub>l<sub>acemen</sub>t <sub>spec</sub>ifi<sub>c</sub>it<sub>y, an</sub>d <sub>norma</sub>l<sub>-</sub> i<sub>ze</sub>d <sub>promp</sub>t <sub>cos</sub>t<sub>, respec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y.</sub> Th<sub>e compos</sub>it<sub>e score</sub> i<sub>s</sub>

$$

F<sub>or</sub> <sub>any</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>w</sub>ith <sub>a</sub>t l<sub>eas</sub>t <sub>one</sub> f<sub>eas</sub>ibl<sub>e</sub> <sub>recor</sub>d <sub>ac</sub>ti<sub>on,</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>s</sub>t<sub>op</sub> <sub>marg</sub>i<sub>n</sub>

$$

$$
\begin{array} { r } { p ^ { \star } ( a \mid s ) = \mathrm { s o f t m a x } _ { a \in {  { \mathcal A } } _ { \mathrm { l a b } } ( s ) } \left( R _ { i } ^ { \mathrm { n e t } } ( s , a ) / T _ { r } \right) , } \end{array}\tag{26}

$$

## 3<sub>.</sub>1 E<sub>xpe</sub>rim<sub>e</sub>nt<sub>a</sub>l S<sub>e</sub>t<sub>up</sub>

D<sub>a</sub>t<sub>ase</sub>t <sub>an</sub>d E<sub>va</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> O<sub>ur exper</sub>i<sub>men</sub>t<sub>s use</sub> th<sub>e</sub> L<sub>a</sub>MP benchmark (Salemi et al. 2024a), which covers six <sub>p</sub>erson-<sub>a</sub>li<sub>ze</sub>d t<sub>as</sub>k<sub>s,</sub> i<sub>nc</sub>l<sub>u</sub>di<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>ree c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub>ifi<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> t<sub>as</sub>k<sub>s—</sub>L<sub>a</sub>MP<sub>-</sub>1 (Citation Identification, binar<sub>y</sub>), LaMP-2 (Movie Ta<sub>gg</sub>in<sub>g</sub>, multiclass with 15 cate<sub>g</sub>ories), and LaMP-3 (Product Ratin<sub>g</sub>, ordinal from 1–5 stars)and four text <sub>g</sub>eneration tasks: LaMP-4 (News Headline Generation), LaMP-5 (Scholarl<sub>y</sub> Title Generation), and LaMP-7 (Tweet Para<sub>p</sub>hrasin<sub>g</sub>).

Followin<sub>g p</sub>revious work (Salemi et al., 2024b,a; Shi et al., 2025), we re<sub>p</sub>ort accurac<sub>y</sub>(Acc.) and F1 score for LaMP-1 and LaMP-2, mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) for LaMP-3. We evaluate text <sub>g</sub>ener-<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on per</sub>f<sub>ormance on</sub> L<sub>a</sub>MP<sub>-</sub>4<sub>,</sub> L<sub>a</sub>MP<sub>-</sub>5<sub>, an</sub>d L<sub>a</sub>MP<sub>-</sub>7 <sub>us</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> ROUGE-1 (R-1) and ROUGE-L (R-L) (Lin, 2004). Dataset statistics, the time-based (T) s<sub>p</sub>lit <sub>p</sub>rotocols, and detailed <sub>me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>n</sub>iti<sub>ons are repor</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> A<sub>ppen</sub>di<sub>x</sub> E<sub>.</sub>1<sub>.</sub>

Implementation Details. We use Qwen3.5-9B(Qwen Team 2026) as the <sub>p</sub>rimar<sub>y</sub> frozen task <sub>g</sub>enerator in all main <sub>exper</sub>i<sub>men</sub>t<sub>s.</sub> W<sub>e repea</sub>t th<sub>e eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on w</sub>ith Ll<sub>ama-</sub>3<sub>.</sub>1<sub>-</sub>8B<sub>-</sub> Instruct(Grattafiori et al. 2024) as an alternative frozen task <sub>g</sub>enerator. Our frozen retriever is Contriever (Izacard et al. 2022), which returns at most M = 20 legal records. Further i<sub>mp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> d<sub>e</sub>t<sub>a</sub>il<sub>s are prov</sub>id<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> A<sub>ppen</sub>di<sub>x</sub> E<sub>.</sub>2<sub>.</sub>

B<sub>ase</sub>li<sub>nes</sub> Th<sub>e ma</sub>i<sub>n compar</sub>i<sub>son con</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>ns e</sub>i<sub>g</sub>ht <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eva</sub>l<sub>-</sub> au<sub>g</sub>mented baselines. BM25 (Robertson and Zara<sub>g</sub>oza 2009) and Contriever (Izacard et al. 2022) <sub>p</sub>rovide s<sub>p</sub>arse and frozen dense relevance rankin<sub>g</sub>s. RankGPT (Sun et al. 2023) <sub>p</sub>erf<sub>orms zero-s</sub>h<sub>o</sub>t li<sub>s</sub>t<sub>w</sub>i<sub>se permu</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on reran</sub>ki<sub>ng, w</sub>h<sub>ereas</sub> ICR (Chen et al. 2025) derives calibrated relevance scores from quer<sub>y</sub>-to-record attention. IC-RALM (Ram et al. 2023) in-<sub>corpora</sub>t<sub>es</sub> <sub>recor</sub>d<sub>s</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h d<sub>eco</sub>di<sub>ng-</sub>ti<sub>me</sub> <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ex</sub>t <sub>sw</sub>it<sub>c</sub>h<sub>-</sub> in<sub>g</sub>, while REPLUG (Shi et al. 2024) mar<sub>g</sub>inalizes token <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> <sub>recor</sub>d<sub>-con</sub>diti<sub>one</sub>d <sub>genera</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>reams</sub> <sub>us-</sub> in<sub>g</sub> normalized retrieval scores. RSPG-Pre (Salemi et al.

2024b) uses a Lon<sub>g</sub>former selector before <sub>g</sub>eneration to choose amon<sub>g</sub> multi<sub>p</sub>le retrievers. PURPLE (Du et al. 2026) l<sub>earns an or</sub>d<sub>er-sens</sub>iti<sub>ve</sub> Pl<sub>ac</sub>k<sub>e</sub>tt<sub>–</sub>L<sub>uce pro</sub>fil<sub>e po</sub>li<sub>cy us</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>rozen</sub> <sub>genera</sub>t<sub>or</sub>’<sub>s</sub> <sub>re</sub>f<sub>erence-response</sub> l<sub>og-</sub>lik<sub>e</sub>lih<sub>oo</sub>d <sub>as</sub> <sub>rewar</sub>d<sub>.</sub> D<sub>e</sub>t<sub>a</sub>il<sub>e</sub>d b<sub>ase</sub>li<sub>ne</sub> <sub>con</sub>fi<sub>gura</sub>ti<sub>ons,</sub> <sub>s</sub>h<sub>are</sub>d b<sub>u</sub>d<sub>ge</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>promp</sub>t<sub>s are prov</sub>id<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> A<sub>ppen</sub>di<sub>x</sub> E<sub>.</sub>2<sub>.</sub>

## D<sub>.</sub>1 Pr<sub>opos</sub>iti<sub>o</sub>n 1<sub>:</sub> E<sub>xac</sub>t<sub>-va</sub>l<sub>ue</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>opp</sub>in<sub>g</sub>

Proof. Fix an instance i and a reachable state s whose current ordered profile prefix is S. Let $\mathscr { L } _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } )$ b<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e se</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f t<sub>erm</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l profile prefixes reachable from S, including S itself via the Stop action. Because C<sub>i</sub> is finite, records cannot repeat, and <sub>pro</sub>fil<sub>e</sub> l<sub>eng</sub>th i<sub>s a</sub>t <sub>mos</sub>t $K _ { i }$ <sub>, every can</sub>did<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e su</sub>bt<sub>ree</sub> i<sub>s</sub> fi<sub>-</sub> nite. Let h(S) denote the maximum number of record-action edges on a path starting from S.

W<sub>e</sub> fi<sub>rs</sub>t <sub>es</sub>t<sub>a</sub>bli<sub>s</sub>h<sub>,</sub> b<sub>y</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>uc</sub>ti<sub>on on</sub> $h ( \mathbf { S } )$ <sub>,</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t

$$
V _ { i } ^ { \star } ( \mathbf { S } ) = \operatorname* { m a x } _ { \mathbf { L } \in \mathcal { L } _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } ) } J _ { i } ^ { \lambda } ( \mathbf { L } ) .\tag{34}
$$

If $h ( \mathbf { S } ) = 0 .$ <sub>, no recor</sub>d <sub>ac</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>s</sub> f<sub>eas</sub>ibl<sub>e, so</sub> ${ \mathcal { L } } _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } ) = \{ \mathbf { S } \}$ <sub>an</sub>d th<sub>e</sub> b<sub>oun</sub>d<sub>ary</sub> <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>on</sub> <sub>g</sub>i<sub>ves</sub> $V _ { i } { } ^ { \star } ( { \bf S } ) = J _ { i } ^ { \lambda } ( { \bf \dot { S } } )$ <sub>.</sub> No<sub>w</sub> <sup>s</sup>upp<sup>ose</sup> $h ( \mathbf { S } ) > 0$ and that Eq. (34) holds for ever<sub>y</sub> child <sub>pre</sub>fi<sub>x.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>erm</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l d<sub>escen</sub>d<sub>an</sub>t<sub>s</sub> d<sub>ecompose as</sub>

$$
\mathcal { L } _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } ) = \{ \mathbf { S } \} \cup \bigcup _ { d \in \mathcal { A } ( s ) \setminus \{ \mathrm { S r o p } \} } \mathcal { L } _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } \oplus d ) .\tag{35}
$$

Eq. (20) <sub>y</sub>ields

$$
\operatorname* { m a x } _ { \mathbf { L } \in \mathscr { L } _ { i } ( \mathbf { S } ) } J _ { i } ^ { \lambda } ( \mathbf { L } ) = \operatorname* { m a x } \left\{ J _ { i } ^ { \lambda } ( \mathbf { S } ) , \operatorname* { m a x } _ { d \in \mathscr { A } ( s ) \backslash \{ \mathrm { S r o p } \} } V _ { i } ^ { \star } ( \mathbf { S } \oplus d ) \right\}\tag{36}
$$

$$
{ \bf \Xi } = V _ { i } ^ { \star } ( { \bf S } ) ,\tag{37}
$$

which <sub>p</sub>roves Eq. (34).

$$

$$
